{"etymology_number":"3","forms":[{"form":"ar","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"arh","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"particle","senses":[{"glosses":["Marks a yes–no tag question prompting the listener to clarify or confirm something."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["Singapore"],"word":"hah"},{"word":"is it"},{"word":"izzit"}],"tags":["interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"How do you delete this row ah?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Reinforces a short, non-rhetorical wh-question."],"tags":["interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Don’t drink and drive ah...","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Emphasizes the need for absolute confirmation, permission or acknowledgment."],"synonyms":[{"word":"hor"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"The drilling upstairs ah, non-stop leh.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A particle indicating the topic of a sentence from its comment."]},{"glosses":["A confirmative final particle used in the middle of a sentence to ascertain the continued attention of the listener."],"synonyms":[{"word":"right"}]},{"glosses":["Tagged at the end of non-interrogative sentence to convey a sense of informality or resignation."],"tags":["uncommon"]},{"glosses":["A vocative particle, used for direct address (over telephone calls, or to directly address the listener)."]},{"glosses":["Used as an intensifier in fixed expressions, sometimes exhortative in meaning."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɑ(ː)/","tags":["Malaysia","Singapore"]},{"ipa":"[ä(ː)~ɐ(ː)]","tags":["Malaysia","Singapore"]}],"upstream_line":105573,"upstream_offset":521373892,"upstream_record_sha256":"0014d7c881cdce1038713414205abcb4ae1b6eda8e2ab478ef7583607e247c9a","word":"ah"}

{"forms":[{"form":"thirsts","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"We developed a great thirst during our hike.","type":"example"},{"text":"He had quite a thirst on and so headed to his favourite Dublin pub.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A sensation of dryness in the throat associated with a craving for liquids, produced by deprivation of drink, or by some other cause (such as fear, excitement, etc.) which stops the secretion of the pharyngeal mucous membrane."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"If you go out in the desert for long without water, the thirst can drive you mad.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The condition producing the sensation of thirst."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She had a real thirst for news and that greatly helped her career.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A want and eager desire (for something); a craving or longing."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He had a thirst for her and she had the same for him.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Sexual lust."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"thûrst"},{"ipa":"/θɜːst/","tags":["Australia","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/θɝst/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/θɪɹst/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/θøːst/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/θeːst/"},{"ipa":"/θɛːst/"},{"ipa":"/fɜːst/"}],"synonyms":[{"tags":["figuratively"],"word":"craving"},{"tags":["figuratively"],"word":"longing"}],"upstream_line":240359,"upstream_offset":1191532072,"upstream_record_sha256":"bb98961e5dddae9129f155692de8a8f00e3f5f3ef2d2a5e2899bcd0bbdee9a03","word":"thirst"}
